The timeline and list of posts;
Code snippets for reference of the how the Creator Inspiration Top posts timeline is so you can enter each post and scrape either verified or none verified or both commenters usernames and push to the database...also push the post ID to scraped_posts so you don't scrape commenters of the same post again.
